2002-0001 ResultPathNotFound Cannot find the target in the specified path. The path specified in the nn::fs function could not be found.
If this error is unexpected, it is possible that either file system operations were not performed according to design, or the appropriate data was not prepared.

In the development environment, if you enable the FS access log you can check whether the nn::fs function call was performed according to design.
For more information about the FS access log output feature, see NintendoSDK Documents > Features > Basic Features > fs Library > Debugging > Access Log Output Feature.

If the expected path is not found for the ROM file system or the downloadable content, check whether authoring was performed appropriately.
For more information about authoring, see NintendoSDK Documents > Features > Building > Creating Application Images, or see NintendoSDK Documents > Features > System> aoc Library > Creating Downloadable Content.		 This error code error message is not defined for this HOME Menu implementation.
If you try to display this error code, the default error message (2999-9999) is displayed.
2002-0002 ResultPathAlreadyExists The target already exists. The target specified by the nn::fs function already exists.
If this error is unexpected, file system operations might not have been performed according to design.

In the development environment, if you enable the FS access log you can check whether the nn::fs function call was performed according to design.
For more information about the FS access log output feature, see NintendoSDK Documents > Features > Basic Features > fs Library > Debugging > Access Log Output Feature.		 This error code error message is not defined for this HOME Menu implementation.
If you try to display this error code, the default error message (2999-9999) is displayed.
2002-0007 ResultTargetLocked The target is locked. The target specified by the nn::fs function is in use.
If this error is unexpected, file system operations might not have been performed according to design.

In the development environment, if you enable the FS access log you can check whether the nn::fs function call was performed according to design.
For more information about the FS access log output feature, see NintendoSDK Documents > Features > Basic Features > fs Library > Debugging > Access Log Output Feature.

For file system operations on the host PC, this error occurs if the operations conflict with virus scans and the like.		 This error code error message is not defined for this HOME Menu implementation.
If you try to display this error code, the default error message (2999-9999) is displayed.
2002-0008 ResultDirectoryNotEmpty There are entries in the target directory. Some entries in the directory are specified by the nn::fs function.
If this error is unexpected, file system operations might not have been performed according to design.

In the development environment, if you enable the FS access log you can check whether the nn::fs function call was performed according to design.
For more information about the FS access log output feature, see NintendoSDK Documents > Features > Basic Features > fs Library > Debugging > Access Log Output Feature.

If you want to recursively delete the directory, including child entries, call the nn::fs::DeleteDirectoryRecursively() function.		 This error code error message is not defined for this HOME Menu implementation.
If you try to display this error code, the default error message (2999-9999) is displayed.

In the development environment, if you enable the FS access log you can check whether the nn::fs function call was performed according to design.

If you try to display this error code, the default error message (2999-9999) is displayed.
2002-0030 ResultUsableSpaceNotEnough Not enough free space. An attempt was made using file system operations to write to or allocate more free space than was available.

You must consider situations where this error might occur for the nn::fs::EnsureSaveData(), nn::fs::ExtendSaveData(), and nn::fs::CreateCacheStorage() functions.
For more information, see NintendoSDK Documents > Features > Basic Features > fs Library > Handling Errors.

If there is not enough free space in the save data file system, temporary storage file system, or cache storage file system, and this is unexpected, file system operations might not have been performed according to design, or the data save area might not have been set to the appropriate size.
For more information about setting the size of the data save area, see NintendoSDK Documents > Features > Basic Features > fs Library > Manual > Features, and NintendoSDK Documents > Features > Building > Creating Application Images > Writing the NMETA File.

If there is not enough free space in RamDisk and this is unexpected, it is possible that either file system operations were not performed according to design, or the buffer was not set to the appropriate size.
For more information, see the NintendoSDK API references for nn::fs::MountRamDisk() and nn::fs::GetRamDiskBufferSize().

In the development environment, if you enable the FS access log you can check which file system operation involved insufficient free space.
For more information about the FS access log output feature, see NintendoSDK Documents > Features > Basic Features > fs Library > Debugging > Access Log Output Feature.		 Unable to save data. 2002-0039 ResultUsableSpaceNotEnoughSdCard Insufficient free space on the SD card. Not enough free space on the SD card.
From the HOME Menu, select Settings > Data Management and resolve the problem by organizing the data so there is enough free space.

If this step does not resolve the problem, check the following.
• Is the SD card inserted correctly?
　Turn the power off and re-insert the SD card.
• Is the SD card terminal part contaminated?
　If it is contaminated, wipe it of with a soft, dry cloth such as a glasses cleaning cloth. Wipe gently so as not to damage the terminals.
• Consider formatting the SD card.
　From the HOME Menu, select Settings > System > Formatting Options > Format microSD Card to format the SD card.
　If you format the SD card, all of the saved data on it is deleted. As needed, move screenshots and video data to the system memory or a PC in advance.
　The save data is saved in the system memory instead of on the SD card.
• If the preceding measures do not resolve the issue, there may be a problem with the SD card itself. Consider replacing the SD card.
　If replacing the SD card does not resolve the issue, the console might be defective.		 Not enough free space on the microSD card.

Please archive data from  (System Settings) ⇒ Data Management on the HOME Menu.		 No hay espacio suficiente en la tarjeta microSD.

Para archivar o borrar datos, abre el menú HOME, accede a la configuración de la consola () y selecciona "Gestión de datos".		 Espace libre insuffisant sur la carte microSD.

Veuillez archiver ou effacer les données dont vous n'avez plus besoin en sélectionnant  (Paramètres de la console) dans le menu HOME, puis Gestion des données.		 Não há espaço suficiente no cartão microSD.

as configurações do console () no menu HOME
e selecione "Gerenciar dados".		 There is not enough free space on the microSD card.

Please archive or delete data from  (System Settings) ⇒ Data Management on the HOME Menu.		 L'espace libre sur la carte microSD est insuffisant.

2002-0050 ResultUnsupportedSdkVersion Unsupported SDK version. This error code error message is not defined for this HOME Menu implementation.
If you try to display this error code, the default error message (2999-9999) is displayed.
2002-0060 ResultMountNameAlreadyExists The mount name already exists or HostRoot is already mounted. Either the mount name specified by the Mount function of the nn::fs API is already in use, or HostRoot is already mounted.
If this error is unexpected, file system operations might not have been performed according to design.

In the development environment, if you enable the FS access log you can check whether the nn::fs function call was performed according to design.
For more information about the FS access log output feature, see NintendoSDK Documents > Features > Basic Features > fs Library > Debugging > Access Log Output Feature.		 This error code error message is not defined for this HOME Menu implementation.
If you try to display this error code, the default error message (2999-9999) is displayed.

If this error is unexpected, file system operations might not have been performed according to design.

In the development environment, if you enable the FS access log you can check whether the nn::fs function call was performed according to design.
For more information about the FS access log output feature, see NintendoSDK Documents > Features > Basic Features > fs Library > Debugging > Access Log Output Feature.		 This error code error message is not defined for this HOME Menu implementation.

2002-1001 ResultPartitionNotFound Cannot find the target partition. This error code error message is not defined for this HOME Menu implementation.
If you try to display this error code, the default error message (2999-9999) is displayed.
2002-1002 ResultTargetNotFound Cannot find the target. Could not find the target for file system operations.

Here are some examples of operations that result in this error and the causes.
・If this error occurs as a result of operations on the host file system, on the ROM file system when the application is running in Raw format (NSPD), or on the save data file system or cache storage file system after the nn::fs::SetSaveDataRootPath() function has run, the reason might be that communication with the host PC has been lost.
・If this error occurs as a result of operations on the save data file system and the cache storage file system when the application is running in Raw format (NSPD), the reason might be that the meta0.ncd directory in the target NSPD directory contains the CNMT files for multiple application IDs.

In the development environment, if you enable the FS access log you might be able to confirm which operation target could not be found.
For more information about the FS access log output feature, see NintendoSDK Documents > Features > Basic Features > fs Library > Debugging > Access Log Output Feature.		 This error code error message is not defined for this HOME Menu implementation.
If you try to display this error code, the default error message (2999-9999) is displayed.
2002-1003 ResultHasNotGottenPatrolCount PatrolCount has not yet been obtained. This error code error message is not defined for this HOME Menu implementation.
If you try to display this error code, the default error message (2999-9999) is displayed.
2002-1004 ResultNcaExternalKeyUnavailable Cannot use the NCA external key. This error code error message is not defined for this HOME Menu implementation.
If you try to display this error code, the default error message (2999-9999) is displayed.
2002-2000 ResultSdCardAccessFailed Failed to access the SD card. Failed to access the SD card.
Check the following.
• Is the SD card inserted correctly?
　Turn the power off and re-insert the SD card.
• Is the SD card terminal part contaminated?
　If it is contaminated, wipe it of with a soft, dry cloth such as a glasses cleaning cloth. Wipe gently so as not to damage the terminals.
• Consider formatting the SD card.
　From the HOME Menu, select Settings > System > Formatting Options > Format microSD Card to format the SD card.
　If you format the SD card, all of the saved data on it is deleted. As needed, move screenshots and video data to the system memory or a PC in advance.
　The save data is saved in the system memory instead of on the SD card.
• If the preceding measures do not resolve the issue, there may be a problem with the SD card itself. Consider replacing the SD card.
　If replacing the SD card does not resolve the issue, the console might be defective.

Alternatively, excessive access to the storage media might have caused malfunctioning or data corruption.
For more information about access restrictions on storage media, see Guidelines > File system > Storage media > Limits on frequency and amount of writing to storage media, and also Limit on frequency of reading storage media.		 Unable to access microSD card.

Please turn off the console and reinsert
the microSD card.

Apaga la consola y vuelve a insertar la tarjeta.

el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 L'accès à la carte microSD a échoué.

o botão POWER por três segundos
e selecione "Opções de desligar".		 Unable to access microSD card.

Please turn off the console and reinsert
the microSD card.

You can turn the power off from Power Options in the Power Menu (hold the POWER Button for three seconds).		 L'accès à la carte microSD a échoué.

Para apagar la consola, mantén pulsado
el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 Impossibile accedere alla scheda microSD.

Spegni la console e reinserisci la scheda microSD.

Per spegnere la console, seleziona Opzioni di spegnimento nel menu Power (tieni premuto il pulsante POWER per tre secondi).		 De microSD-kaart is niet toegankelijk.

Zet het systeem uit en voer de microSD-kaart opnieuw in.

Je kunt het systeem uitzetten via OPNIEUW OPSTARTEN / UITZETTEN in het POWER-menu (houd de POWER-knop drie seconden ingedrukt).		 Não é possível aceder ao cartão microSD.

Desliga a consola e volta a inserir o cartão microSD.

Podes desligar a consola a partir das opções de energia no menu Power (mantém premido o botão POWER durante três segundos).		 Не удается получить доступ к карте microSD.

Выключите консоль, извлеките и снова вставьте карту microSD.

Вы можете отключить питание в меню электропитания в разделе Параметры электропитания (нажмите и удерживайте кнопку POWER в течение трех секунд).		 SDカードにアクセスできませんでした。

本体の電源をOFFにしてから、SDカードを差しなおしてください。

電源OFFは、電源メニュー（電源ボタンを3秒長押し）の「電源オプション」からおこなえます。		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 無法存取SD卡。

請關閉主機電源後，重新插入SD卡。

關閉電源可到電源選單（長按電源鍵3秒）的「電源選項」執行。		 SD 카드에 액세스하지 못했습니다.

본체 전원을 끄고 나서 SD 카드를 다시 꽂아 주십시오.

전원 OFF는 전원 메뉴(전원 버튼 3초간 누르기)의 「전원 옵션」에서 할 수 있습니다.		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 OK

電源OFFは、電源メニュー（電源ボタンを3秒長押し）の「電源オプション」からおこなえます。		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 無法存取SD卡。

請關閉主機電源後，重新插入SD卡。

關閉電源可到電源選單（長按電源鍵3秒）的「電源選項」執行。		 SD 카드에 액세스하지 못했습니다.

본체 전원을 끄고 나서 SD 카드를 다시 꽂아 주십시오.

전원 OFF는 전원 메뉴(전원 버튼 3초간 누르기)의 「전원 옵션」에서 할 수 있습니다.		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 OK
2002-2004 ResultSdCardAccessFailed Failed to access the SD card. Failed to access the SD card.
Check the following.
• Is the SD card inserted correctly?
　Turn the power off and re-insert the SD card.
• Is the SD card terminal part contaminated?
　If it is contaminated, wipe it of with a soft, dry cloth such as a glasses cleaning cloth. Wipe gently so as not to damage the terminals.
• Consider formatting the SD card.
　From the HOME Menu, select Settings > System > Formatting Options > Format microSD Card to format the SD card.
　If you format the SD card, all of the saved data on it is deleted. As needed, move screenshots and video data to the system memory or a PC in advance.
　The save data is saved in the system memory instead of on the SD card.
• If the preceding measures do not resolve the issue, there may be a problem with the SD card itself. Consider replacing the SD card.
　If replacing the SD card does not resolve the issue, the console might be defective.

Alternatively, excessive access to the storage media might have caused malfunctioning or data corruption.
For more information about access restrictions on storage media, see Guidelines > File system > Storage media > Limits on frequency and amount of writing to storage media, and also Limit on frequency of reading storage media.		 Unable to access microSD card.

Please turn off the console and reinsert
the microSD card.

You can turn the power off from Power Options in the Power Menu (hold the POWER Button for three seconds).		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta microSD.

Apaga la consola y vuelve a insertar la tarjeta.

Para apagar la consola, mantén oprimido
el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 L'accès à la carte microSD a échoué.

Éteignez la console, puis retirez et réinsérez la carte microSD.

Vous pouvez éteindre la console depuis les options d'alimentation, dans le menu d'alimentation (maintenez le bouton d'alimentation trois secondes pour l'ouvrir).		 Não foi possível acessar o cartão microSD.

Desligue o console e insira o cartão novamente.

Para desligar o console, aperte e segure
o botão POWER por três segundos
e selecione "Opções de desligar".		 Unable to access microSD card.

Please turn off the console and reinsert
the microSD card.

You can turn the power off from Power Options in the Power Menu (hold the POWER Button for three seconds).		 L'accès à la carte microSD a échoué.

Éteignez la console, puis retirez et réinsérez la carte microSD.

Vous pouvez éteindre la console depuis les options d'alimentation, dans le menu d'alimentation (maintenez le bouton d'alimentation trois secondes pour l'ouvrir).		 Es konnte nicht auf die microSD Card zugegriffen werden.

Bitte schalte die Konsole aus, entferne die microSD Card und stecke sie erneut ein.

Du kannst die Konsole in den Power-Optionen im POWER-Menü ausschalten (POWER-Knopf 3 Sekunden gedrückt halten).		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta microSD.

Apaga la consola y vuelve a insertar la tarjeta.

Para apagar la consola, mantén pulsado
el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 Impossibile accedere alla scheda microSD.

Spegni la console e reinserisci la scheda microSD.

Per spegnere la console, seleziona Opzioni di spegnimento nel menu Power (tieni premuto il pulsante POWER per tre secondi).		 De microSD-kaart is niet toegankelijk.

Zet het systeem uit en voer de microSD-kaart opnieuw in.

Je kunt het systeem uitzetten via OPNIEUW OPSTARTEN / UITZETTEN in het POWER-menu (houd de POWER-knop drie seconden ingedrukt).		 Não é possível aceder ao cartão microSD.

Desliga a consola e volta a inserir o cartão microSD.

Podes desligar a consola a partir das opções de energia no menu Power (mantém premido o botão POWER durante três segundos).		 Не удается получить доступ к карте microSD.

Выключите консоль, извлеките и снова вставьте карту microSD.

Please turn off the console and reinsert
the microSD card.

You can turn the power off from Power Options in the Power Menu (hold the POWER Button for three seconds).		 L'accès à la carte microSD a échoué.

Éteignez la console, puis retirez et réinsérez la carte microSD.

Vous pouvez éteindre la console depuis les options d'alimentation, dans le menu d'alimentation (maintenez le bouton d'alimentation trois secondes pour l'ouvrir).		 Es konnte nicht auf die microSD Card zugegriffen werden.

Bitte schalte die Konsole aus, entferne die microSD Card und stecke sie erneut ein.

Du kannst die Konsole in den Power-Optionen im POWER-Menü ausschalten (POWER-Knopf 3 Sekunden gedrückt halten).		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta microSD.

Apaga la consola y vuelve a insertar la tarjeta.

Para apagar la consola, mantén pulsado
el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 Impossibile accedere alla scheda microSD.

Spegni la console e reinserisci la scheda microSD.

Per spegnere la console, seleziona Opzioni di spegnimento nel menu Power (tieni premuto il pulsante POWER per tre secondi).		 De microSD-kaart is niet toegankelijk.

Zet het systeem uit en voer de microSD-kaart opnieuw in.

Je kunt het systeem uitzetten via OPNIEUW OPSTARTEN / UITZETTEN in het POWER-menu (houd de POWER-knop drie seconden ingedrukt).		 Não é possível aceder ao cartão microSD.

Desliga a consola e volta a inserir o cartão microSD.

Podes desligar a consola a partir das opções de energia no menu Power (mantém premido o botão POWER durante três segundos).		 Не удается получить доступ к карте microSD.

Выключите консоль, извлеките и снова вставьте карту microSD.

Вы можете отключить питание в меню электропитания в разделе Параметры электропитания (нажмите и удерживайте кнопку POWER в течение трех секунд).		 SDカードにアクセスできませんでした。

本体の電源をOFFにしてから、SDカードを差しなおしてください。

電源OFFは、電源メニュー（電源ボタンを3秒長押し）の「電源オプション」からおこなえます。		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 無法存取SD卡。

請關閉主機電源後，重新插入SD卡。

關閉電源可到電源選單（長按電源鍵3秒）的「電源選項」執行。		 SD 카드에 액세스하지 못했습니다.

본체 전원을 끄고 나서 SD 카드를 다시 꽂아 주십시오.

전원 OFF는 전원 메뉴(전원 버튼 3초간 누르기)의 「전원 옵션」에서 할 수 있습니다.		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 OK
2002-2037 ResultSdCardAccessFailed Failed to access the SD card. Failed to access the SD card.
Check the following.
• Is the SD card inserted correctly?
　Turn the power off and re-insert the SD card.
• Is the SD card terminal part contaminated?
　If it is contaminated, wipe it of with a soft, dry cloth such as a glasses cleaning cloth. Wipe gently so as not to damage the terminals.
• Consider formatting the SD card.
　From the HOME Menu, select Settings > System > Formatting Options > Format microSD Card to format the SD card.
　If you format the SD card, all of the saved data on it is deleted. As needed, move screenshots and video data to the system memory or a PC in advance.
　The save data is saved in the system memory instead of on the SD card.
• If the preceding measures do not resolve the issue, there may be a problem with the SD card itself. Consider replacing the SD card.
　If replacing the SD card does not resolve the issue, the console might be defective.

Alternatively, excessive access to the storage media might have caused malfunctioning or data corruption.
For more information about access restrictions on storage media, see Guidelines > File system > Storage media > Limits on frequency and amount of writing to storage media, and also Limit on frequency of reading storage media.		 Unable to access microSD card.

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 OK
2002-2041 ResultSdCardAccessFailed Failed to access the SD card. Failed to access the SD card.
Check the following.
• Is the SD card inserted correctly?
　Turn the power off and re-insert the SD card.
• Is the SD card terminal part contaminated?
　If it is contaminated, wipe it of with a soft, dry cloth such as a glasses cleaning cloth. Wipe gently so as not to damage the terminals.
• Consider formatting the SD card.
　From the HOME Menu, select Settings > System > Formatting Options > Format microSD Card to format the SD card.
　If you format the SD card, all of the saved data on it is deleted. As needed, move screenshots and video data to the system memory or a PC in advance.
　The save data is saved in the system memory instead of on the SD card.
• If the preceding measures do not resolve the issue, there may be a problem with the SD card itself. Consider replacing the SD card.
　If replacing the SD card does not resolve the issue, the console might be defective.

Alternatively, excessive access to the storage media might have caused malfunctioning or data corruption.
For more information about access restrictions on storage media, see Guidelines > File system > Storage media > Limits on frequency and amount of writing to storage media, and also Limit on frequency of reading storage media.		 Unable to access microSD card.

Please turn off the console and reinsert
the microSD card.

You can turn the power off from Power Options in the Power Menu (hold the POWER Button for three seconds).		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta microSD.

Apaga la consola y vuelve a insertar la tarjeta.

Para apagar la consola, mantén oprimido
el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 L'accès à la carte microSD a échoué.

Éteignez la console, puis retirez et réinsérez la carte microSD.

Vous pouvez éteindre la console depuis les options d'alimentation, dans le menu d'alimentation (maintenez le bouton d'alimentation trois secondes pour l'ouvrir).		 Não foi possível acessar o cartão microSD.

Desligue o console e insira o cartão novamente.

Para desligar o console, aperte e segure
o botão POWER por três segundos
e selecione "Opções de desligar".		 Unable to access microSD card.

Please turn off the console and reinsert
the microSD card.

You can turn the power off from Power Options in the Power Menu (hold the POWER Button for three seconds).		 L'accès à la carte microSD a échoué.

Éteignez la console, puis retirez et réinsérez la carte microSD.

Vous pouvez éteindre la console depuis les options d'alimentation, dans le menu d'alimentation (maintenez le bouton d'alimentation trois secondes pour l'ouvrir).		 Es konnte nicht auf die microSD Card zugegriffen werden.

Bitte schalte die Konsole aus, entferne die microSD Card und stecke sie erneut ein.

Du kannst die Konsole in den Power-Optionen im POWER-Menü ausschalten (POWER-Knopf 3 Sekunden gedrückt halten).		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta microSD.

Apaga la consola y vuelve a insertar la tarjeta.

Para apagar la consola, mantén pulsado
el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 Impossibile accedere alla scheda microSD.

Spegni la console e reinserisci la scheda microSD.

Per spegnere la console, seleziona Opzioni di spegnimento nel menu Power (tieni premuto il pulsante POWER per tre secondi).		 De microSD-kaart is niet toegankelijk.

Zet het systeem uit en voer de microSD-kaart opnieuw in.

Je kunt het systeem uitzetten via OPNIEUW OPSTARTEN / UITZETTEN in het POWER-menu (houd de POWER-knop drie seconden ingedrukt).		 Não é possível aceder ao cartão microSD.

Desliga a consola e volta a inserir o cartão microSD.

Podes desligar a consola a partir das opções de energia no menu Power (mantém premido o botão POWER durante três segundos).		 Не удается получить доступ к карте microSD.

Выключите консоль, извлеките и снова вставьте карту microSD.

Вы можете отключить питание в меню электропитания в разделе Параметры электропитания (нажмите и удерживайте кнопку POWER в течение трех секунд).		 SDカードにアクセスできませんでした。

本体の電源をOFFにしてから、SDカードを差しなおしてください。

電源OFFは、電源メニュー（電源ボタンを3秒長押し）の「電源オプション」からおこなえます。		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 無法存取SD卡。

請關閉主機電源後，重新插入SD卡。

關閉電源可到電源選單（長按電源鍵3秒）的「電源選項」執行。		 SD 카드에 액세스하지 못했습니다.

본체 전원을 끄고 나서 SD 카드를 다시 꽂아 주십시오.

전원 OFF는 전원 메뉴(전원 버튼 3초간 누르기)의 「전원 옵션」에서 할 수 있습니다.		 无法读取SD卡。

Je kunt het systeem uitzetten via OPNIEUW OPSTARTEN / UITZETTEN in het POWER-menu (houd de POWER-knop drie seconden ingedrukt).		 Não é possível aceder ao cartão microSD.

Desliga a consola e volta a inserir o cartão microSD.

Podes desligar a consola a partir das opções de energia no menu Power (mantém premido o botão POWER durante três segundos).		 Не удается получить доступ к карте microSD.

Выключите консоль, извлеките и снова вставьте карту microSD.

Вы можете отключить питание в меню электропитания в разделе Параметры электропитания (нажмите и удерживайте кнопку POWER в течение трех секунд).		 SDカードにアクセスできませんでした。

本体の電源をOFFにしてから、SDカードを差しなおしてください。

電源OFFは、電源メニュー（電源ボタンを3秒長押し）の「電源オプション」からおこなえます。		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 無法存取SD卡。

請關閉主機電源後，重新插入SD卡。

關閉電源可到電源選單（長按電源鍵3秒）的「電源選項」執行。		 SD 카드에 액세스하지 못했습니다.

본체 전원을 끄고 나서 SD 카드를 다시 꽂아 주십시오.

전원 OFF는 전원 메뉴(전원 버튼 3초간 누르기)의 「전원 옵션」에서 할 수 있습니다.		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 OK
2002-2046 ResultSdCardAccessFailed Failed to access the SD card. Failed to access the SD card.
Check the following.
• Is the SD card inserted correctly?
　Turn the power off and re-insert the SD card.
• Is the SD card terminal part contaminated?
　If it is contaminated, wipe it of with a soft, dry cloth such as a glasses cleaning cloth. Wipe gently so as not to damage the terminals.
• Consider formatting the SD card.
　From the HOME Menu, select Settings > System > Formatting Options > Format microSD Card to format the SD card.
　If you format the SD card, all of the saved data on it is deleted. As needed, move screenshots and video data to the system memory or a PC in advance.
　The save data is saved in the system memory instead of on the SD card.
• If the preceding measures do not resolve the issue, there may be a problem with the SD card itself. Consider replacing the SD card.
　If replacing the SD card does not resolve the issue, the console might be defective.

Alternatively, excessive access to the storage media might have caused malfunctioning or data corruption.
For more information about access restrictions on storage media, see Guidelines > File system > Storage media > Limits on frequency and amount of writing to storage media, and also Limit on frequency of reading storage media.		 Unable to access microSD card.

Please turn off the console and reinsert
the microSD card.

You can turn the power off from Power Options in the Power Menu (hold the POWER Button for three seconds).		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta microSD.

Apaga la consola y vuelve a insertar la tarjeta.

Para apagar la consola, mantén oprimido
el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 L'accès à la carte microSD a échoué.

Éteignez la console, puis retirez et réinsérez la carte microSD.

Vous pouvez éteindre la console depuis les options d'alimentation, dans le menu d'alimentation (maintenez le bouton d'alimentation trois secondes pour l'ouvrir).		 Não foi possível acessar o cartão microSD.

o botão POWER por três segundos
e selecione "Opções de desligar".		 Unable to access microSD card.

You can turn the power off from Power Options in the Power Menu (hold the POWER Button for three seconds).		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta microSD.

Apaga la consola y vuelve a insertar la tarjeta.

Para apagar la consola, mantén oprimido
el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 L'accès à la carte microSD a échoué.

Éteignez la console, puis retirez et réinsérez la carte microSD.

Vous pouvez éteindre la console depuis les options d'alimentation, dans le menu d'alimentation (maintenez le bouton d'alimentation trois secondes pour l'ouvrir).		 Não foi possível acessar o cartão microSD.

Desligue o console e insira o cartão novamente.

Para desligar o console, aperte e segure
o botão POWER por três segundos
e selecione "Opções de desligar".		 Unable to access microSD card.

Please turn off the console and reinsert
the microSD card.

You can turn the power off from Power Options in the Power Menu (hold the POWER Button for three seconds).		 L'accès à la carte microSD a échoué.

Éteignez la console, puis retirez et réinsérez la carte microSD.

Vous pouvez éteindre la console depuis les options d'alimentation, dans le menu d'alimentation (maintenez le bouton d'alimentation trois secondes pour l'ouvrir).		 Es konnte nicht auf die microSD Card zugegriffen werden.

Bitte schalte die Konsole aus, entferne die microSD Card und stecke sie erneut ein.

Du kannst die Konsole in den Power-Optionen im POWER-Menü ausschalten (POWER-Knopf 3 Sekunden gedrückt halten).		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta microSD.

Apaga la consola y vuelve a insertar la tarjeta.

Para apagar la consola, mantén pulsado
el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 Impossibile accedere alla scheda microSD.

Spegni la console e reinserisci la scheda microSD.

Per spegnere la console, seleziona Opzioni di spegnimento nel menu Power (tieni premuto il pulsante POWER per tre secondi).		 De microSD-kaart is niet toegankelijk.

Zet het systeem uit en voer de microSD-kaart opnieuw in.

Je kunt het systeem uitzetten via OPNIEUW OPSTARTEN / UITZETTEN in het POWER-menu (houd de POWER-knop drie seconden ingedrukt).		 Não é possível aceder ao cartão microSD.

Desliga a consola e volta a inserir o cartão microSD.

Podes desligar a consola a partir das opções de energia no menu Power (mantém premido o botão POWER durante três segundos).		 Не удается получить доступ к карте microSD.

Выключите консоль, извлеките и снова вставьте карту microSD.

Вы можете отключить питание в меню электропитания в разделе Параметры электропитания (нажмите и удерживайте кнопку POWER в течение трех секунд).		 SDカードにアクセスできませんでした。

本体の電源をOFFにしてから、SDカードを差しなおしてください。

電源OFFは、電源メニュー（電源ボタンを3秒長押し）の「電源オプション」からおこなえます。		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 無法存取SD卡。

請關閉主機電源後，重新插入SD卡。

關閉電源可到電源選單（長按電源鍵3秒）的「電源選項」執行。		 SD 카드에 액세스하지 못했습니다.

본체 전원을 끄고 나서 SD 카드를 다시 꽂아 주십시오.

전원 OFF는 전원 메뉴(전원 버튼 3초간 누르기)의 「전원 옵션」에서 할 수 있습니다.		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 OK
2002-2053 ResultSdCardAccessFailed Failed to access the SD card. Failed to access the SD card.
Check the following.
• Is the SD card inserted correctly?
　Turn the power off and re-insert the SD card.
• Is the SD card terminal part contaminated?
　If it is contaminated, wipe it of with a soft, dry cloth such as a glasses cleaning cloth. Wipe gently so as not to damage the terminals.
• Consider formatting the SD card.
　From the HOME Menu, select Settings > System > Formatting Options > Format microSD Card to format the SD card.
　If you format the SD card, all of the saved data on it is deleted. As needed, move screenshots and video data to the system memory or a PC in advance.
　The save data is saved in the system memory instead of on the SD card.
• If the preceding measures do not resolve the issue, there may be a problem with the SD card itself. Consider replacing the SD card.
　If replacing the SD card does not resolve the issue, the console might be defective.

Alternatively, excessive access to the storage media might have caused malfunctioning or data corruption.
For more information about access restrictions on storage media, see Guidelines > File system > Storage media > Limits on frequency and amount of writing to storage media, and also Limit on frequency of reading storage media.		 Unable to access microSD card.

關閉電源可到電源選單（長按電源鍵3秒）的「電源選項」執行。		 SD 카드에 액세스하지 못했습니다.

본체 전원을 끄고 나서 SD 카드를 다시 꽂아 주십시오.

전원 OFF는 전원 메뉴(전원 버튼 3초간 누르기)의 「전원 옵션」에서 할 수 있습니다.		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 OK
2002-2057 ResultSdCardAccessFailed Failed to access the SD card. Failed to access the SD card.
Check the following.
• Is the SD card inserted correctly?
　Turn the power off and re-insert the SD card.
• Is the SD card terminal part contaminated?
　If it is contaminated, wipe it of with a soft, dry cloth such as a glasses cleaning cloth. Wipe gently so as not to damage the terminals.
• Consider formatting the SD card.
　From the HOME Menu, select Settings > System > Formatting Options > Format microSD Card to format the SD card.
　If you format the SD card, all of the saved data on it is deleted. As needed, move screenshots and video data to the system memory or a PC in advance.
　The save data is saved in the system memory instead of on the SD card.
• If the preceding measures do not resolve the issue, there may be a problem with the SD card itself. Consider replacing the SD card.
　If replacing the SD card does not resolve the issue, the console might be defective.

Alternatively, excessive access to the storage media might have caused malfunctioning or data corruption.
For more information about access restrictions on storage media, see Guidelines > File system > Storage media > Limits on frequency and amount of writing to storage media, and also Limit on frequency of reading storage media.		 Unable to access microSD card.

Please turn off the console and reinsert
the microSD card.

You can turn the power off from Power Options in the Power Menu (hold the POWER Button for three seconds).		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta microSD.

Apaga la consola y vuelve a insertar la tarjeta.

Para apagar la consola, mantén oprimido
el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 L'accès à la carte microSD a échoué.

Éteignez la console, puis retirez et réinsérez la carte microSD.

Vous pouvez éteindre la console depuis les options d'alimentation, dans le menu d'alimentation (maintenez le bouton d'alimentation trois secondes pour l'ouvrir).		 Não foi possível acessar o cartão microSD.

Desligue o console e insira o cartão novamente.

Para desligar o console, aperte e segure
o botão POWER por três segundos
e selecione "Opções de desligar".		 Unable to access microSD card.

Please turn off the console and reinsert
the microSD card.

You can turn the power off from Power Options in the Power Menu (hold the POWER Button for three seconds).		 L'accès à la carte microSD a échoué.

Éteignez la console, puis retirez et réinsérez la carte microSD.

Vous pouvez éteindre la console depuis les options d'alimentation, dans le menu d'alimentation (maintenez le bouton d'alimentation trois secondes pour l'ouvrir).		 Es konnte nicht auf die microSD Card zugegriffen werden.

Bitte schalte die Konsole aus, entferne die microSD Card und stecke sie erneut ein.

Du kannst die Konsole in den Power-Optionen im POWER-Menü ausschalten (POWER-Knopf 3 Sekunden gedrückt halten).		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta microSD.

Apaga la consola y vuelve a insertar la tarjeta.

Para apagar la consola, mantén pulsado
el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 Impossibile accedere alla scheda microSD.

Spegni la console e reinserisci la scheda microSD.

Per spegnere la console, seleziona Opzioni di spegnimento nel menu Power (tieni premuto il pulsante POWER per tre secondi).		 De microSD-kaart is niet toegankelijk.

Zet het systeem uit en voer de microSD-kaart opnieuw in.

Je kunt het systeem uitzetten via OPNIEUW OPSTARTEN / UITZETTEN in het POWER-menu (houd de POWER-knop drie seconden ingedrukt).		 Não é possível aceder ao cartão microSD.

Desliga a consola e volta a inserir o cartão microSD.

Podes desligar a consola a partir das opções de energia no menu Power (mantém premido o botão POWER durante três segundos).		 Не удается получить доступ к карте microSD.

Выключите консоль, извлеките и снова вставьте карту microSD.

Вы можете отключить питание в меню электропитания в разделе Параметры электропитания (нажмите и удерживайте кнопку POWER в течение трех секунд).		 SDカードにアクセスできませんでした。

本体の電源をOFFにしてから、SDカードを差しなおしてください。

電源OFFは、電源メニュー（電源ボタンを3秒長押し）の「電源オプション」からおこなえます。		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

Du kannst die Konsole in den Power-Optionen im POWER-Menü ausschalten (POWER-Knopf 3 Sekunden gedrückt halten).		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta microSD.

Apaga la consola y vuelve a insertar la tarjeta.

Para apagar la consola, mantén pulsado
el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 Impossibile accedere alla scheda microSD.

Spegni la console e reinserisci la scheda microSD.

Per spegnere la console, seleziona Opzioni di spegnimento nel menu Power (tieni premuto il pulsante POWER per tre secondi).		 De microSD-kaart is niet toegankelijk.

Zet het systeem uit en voer de microSD-kaart opnieuw in.

Je kunt het systeem uitzetten via OPNIEUW OPSTARTEN / UITZETTEN in het POWER-menu (houd de POWER-knop drie seconden ingedrukt).		 Não é possível aceder ao cartão microSD.

Desliga a consola e volta a inserir o cartão microSD.

Podes desligar a consola a partir das opções de energia no menu Power (mantém premido o botão POWER durante três segundos).		 Не удается получить доступ к карте microSD.

Выключите консоль, извлеките и снова вставьте карту microSD.

Вы можете отключить питание в меню электропитания в разделе Параметры электропитания (нажмите и удерживайте кнопку POWER в течение трех секунд).		 SDカードにアクセスできませんでした。

本体の電源をOFFにしてから、SDカードを差しなおしてください。

電源OFFは、電源メニュー（電源ボタンを3秒長押し）の「電源オプション」からおこなえます。		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 無法存取SD卡。

請關閉主機電源後，重新插入SD卡。

關閉電源可到電源選單（長按電源鍵3秒）的「電源選項」執行。		 SD 카드에 액세스하지 못했습니다.

본체 전원을 끄고 나서 SD 카드를 다시 꽂아 주십시오.

전원 OFF는 전원 메뉴(전원 버튼 3초간 누르기)의 「전원 옵션」에서 할 수 있습니다.		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 OK
2002-2062 ResultSdCardAccessFailed Failed to access the SD card. Failed to access the SD card.
Check the following.
• Is the SD card inserted correctly?
　Turn the power off and re-insert the SD card.
• Is the SD card terminal part contaminated?
　If it is contaminated, wipe it of with a soft, dry cloth such as a glasses cleaning cloth. Wipe gently so as not to damage the terminals.
• Consider formatting the SD card.
　From the HOME Menu, select Settings > System > Formatting Options > Format microSD Card to format the SD card.
　If you format the SD card, all of the saved data on it is deleted. As needed, move screenshots and video data to the system memory or a PC in advance.
　The save data is saved in the system memory instead of on the SD card.
• If the preceding measures do not resolve the issue, there may be a problem with the SD card itself. Consider replacing the SD card.
　If replacing the SD card does not resolve the issue, the console might be defective.

Alternatively, excessive access to the storage media might have caused malfunctioning or data corruption.
For more information about access restrictions on storage media, see Guidelines > File system > Storage media > Limits on frequency and amount of writing to storage media, and also Limit on frequency of reading storage media.		 Unable to access microSD card.

Please turn off the console and reinsert
the microSD card.

el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 L'accès à la carte microSD a échoué.

　From the HOME Menu, select Settings > System > Formatting Options > Format microSD Card to format the SD card.
　If you format the SD card, all of the saved data on it is deleted. As needed, move screenshots and video data to the system memory or a PC in advance.
　The save data is saved in the system memory instead of on the SD card.
• If the preceding measures do not resolve the issue, there may be a problem with the SD card itself. Consider replacing the SD card.
　If replacing the SD card does not resolve the issue, the console might be defective.

Alternatively, excessive access to the storage media might have caused malfunctioning or data corruption.
For more information about access restrictions on storage media, see Guidelines > File system > Storage media > Limits on frequency and amount of writing to storage media, and also Limit on frequency of reading storage media.		 Unable to access microSD card.

Please turn off the console and reinsert
the microSD card.

You can turn the power off from Power Options in the Power Menu (hold the POWER Button for three seconds).		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta microSD.

Apaga la consola y vuelve a insertar la tarjeta.

Para apagar la consola, mantén oprimido
el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 L'accès à la carte microSD a échoué.

Éteignez la console, puis retirez et réinsérez la carte microSD.

Vous pouvez éteindre la console depuis les options d'alimentation, dans le menu d'alimentation (maintenez le bouton d'alimentation trois secondes pour l'ouvrir).		 Não foi possível acessar o cartão microSD.

Desligue o console e insira o cartão novamente.

Para desligar o console, aperte e segure
o botão POWER por três segundos
e selecione "Opções de desligar".		 Unable to access microSD card.

Please turn off the console and reinsert
the microSD card.

You can turn the power off from Power Options in the Power Menu (hold the POWER Button for three seconds).		 L'accès à la carte microSD a échoué.

Vous pouvez éteindre la console depuis les options d'alimentation, dans le menu d'alimentation (maintenez le bouton d'alimentation trois secondes pour l'ouvrir).		 Es konnte nicht auf die microSD Card zugegriffen werden.

Bitte schalte die Konsole aus, entferne die microSD Card und stecke sie erneut ein.

Du kannst die Konsole in den Power-Optionen im POWER-Menü ausschalten (POWER-Knopf 3 Sekunden gedrückt halten).		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta microSD.

Apaga la consola y vuelve a insertar la tarjeta.

Para apagar la consola, mantén pulsado
el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 Impossibile accedere alla scheda microSD.

Spegni la console e reinserisci la scheda microSD.

Per spegnere la console, seleziona Opzioni di spegnimento nel menu Power (tieni premuto il pulsante POWER per tre secondi).		 De microSD-kaart is niet toegankelijk.

Zet het systeem uit en voer de microSD-kaart opnieuw in.

Je kunt het systeem uitzetten via OPNIEUW OPSTARTEN / UITZETTEN in het POWER-menu (houd de POWER-knop drie seconden ingedrukt).		 Não é possível aceder ao cartão microSD.

Desliga a consola e volta a inserir o cartão microSD.

Podes desligar a consola a partir das opções de energia no menu Power (mantém premido o botão POWER durante três segundos).		 Не удается получить доступ к карте microSD.

Выключите консоль, извлеките и снова вставьте карту microSD.

Вы можете отключить питание в меню электропитания в разделе Параметры электропитания (нажмите и удерживайте кнопку POWER в течение трех секунд).		 SDカードにアクセスできませんでした。

本体の電源をOFFにしてから、SDカードを差しなおしてください。

電源OFFは、電源メニュー（電源ボタンを3秒長押し）の「電源オプション」からおこなえます。		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 無法存取SD卡。

請關閉主機電源後，重新插入SD卡。

關閉電源可到電源選單（長按電源鍵3秒）的「電源選項」執行。		 SD 카드에 액세스하지 못했습니다.

본체 전원을 끄고 나서 SD 카드를 다시 꽂아 주십시오.

전원 OFF는 전원 메뉴(전원 버튼 3초간 누르기)의 「전원 옵션」에서 할 수 있습니다.		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 OK

Вы можете отключить питание в меню электропитания в разделе Параметры электропитания (нажмите и удерживайте кнопку POWER в течение трех секунд).		 SDカードにアクセスできませんでした。

本体の電源をOFFにしてから、SDカードを差しなおしてください。

電源OFFは、電源メニュー（電源ボタンを3秒長押し）の「電源オプション」からおこなえます。		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 無法存取SD卡。

請關閉主機電源後，重新插入SD卡。

關閉電源可到電源選單（長按電源鍵3秒）的「電源選項」執行。		 SD 카드에 액세스하지 못했습니다.

본체 전원을 끄고 나서 SD 카드를 다시 꽂아 주십시오.

전원 OFF는 전원 메뉴(전원 버튼 3초간 누르기)의 「전원 옵션」에서 할 수 있습니다.		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 OK
2002-2078 ResultSdCardAccessFailed Failed to access the SD card. Failed to access the SD card.
Check the following.
• Is the SD card inserted correctly?
　Turn the power off and re-insert the SD card.
• Is the SD card terminal part contaminated?
　If it is contaminated, wipe it of with a soft, dry cloth such as a glasses cleaning cloth. Wipe gently so as not to damage the terminals.
• Consider formatting the SD card.
　From the HOME Menu, select Settings > System > Formatting Options > Format microSD Card to format the SD card.
　If you format the SD card, all of the saved data on it is deleted. As needed, move screenshots and video data to the system memory or a PC in advance.
　The save data is saved in the system memory instead of on the SD card.
• If the preceding measures do not resolve the issue, there may be a problem with the SD card itself. Consider replacing the SD card.
　If replacing the SD card does not resolve the issue, the console might be defective.

Alternatively, excessive access to the storage media might have caused malfunctioning or data corruption.
For more information about access restrictions on storage media, see Guidelines > File system > Storage media > Limits on frequency and amount of writing to storage media, and also Limit on frequency of reading storage media.		 Unable to access microSD card.

Please turn off the console and reinsert
the microSD card.

You can turn the power off from Power Options in the Power Menu (hold the POWER Button for three seconds).		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta microSD.

Apaga la consola y vuelve a insertar la tarjeta.

Para apagar la consola, mantén oprimido
el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 L'accès à la carte microSD a échoué.

Éteignez la console, puis retirez et réinsérez la carte microSD.

Vous pouvez éteindre la console depuis les options d'alimentation, dans le menu d'alimentation (maintenez le bouton d'alimentation trois secondes pour l'ouvrir).		 Não foi possível acessar o cartão microSD.

Desligue o console e insira o cartão novamente.

Para desligar o console, aperte e segure
o botão POWER por três segundos
e selecione "Opções de desligar".		 Unable to access microSD card.

Please turn off the console and reinsert
the microSD card.

You can turn the power off from Power Options in the Power Menu (hold the POWER Button for three seconds).		 L'accès à la carte microSD a échoué.

Éteignez la console, puis retirez et réinsérez la carte microSD.

Vous pouvez éteindre la console depuis les options d'alimentation, dans le menu d'alimentation (maintenez le bouton d'alimentation trois secondes pour l'ouvrir).		 Es konnte nicht auf die microSD Card zugegriffen werden.

Bitte schalte die Konsole aus, entferne die microSD Card und stecke sie erneut ein.

Du kannst die Konsole in den Power-Optionen im POWER-Menü ausschalten (POWER-Knopf 3 Sekunden gedrückt halten).		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta microSD.

Apaga la consola y vuelve a insertar la tarjeta.

Para apagar la consola, mantén pulsado
el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 Impossibile accedere alla scheda microSD.

Spegni la console e reinserisci la scheda microSD.

Per spegnere la console, seleziona Opzioni di spegnimento nel menu Power (tieni premuto il pulsante POWER per tre secondi).		 De microSD-kaart is niet toegankelijk.

Zet het systeem uit en voer de microSD-kaart opnieuw in.

Je kunt het systeem uitzetten via OPNIEUW OPSTARTEN / UITZETTEN in het POWER-menu (houd de POWER-knop drie seconden ingedrukt).		 Não é possível aceder ao cartão microSD.

Vous pouvez éteindre la console depuis les options d'alimentation, dans le menu d'alimentation (maintenez le bouton d'alimentation trois secondes pour l'ouvrir).		 Não foi possível acessar o cartão microSD.

Desligue o console e insira o cartão novamente.

Para desligar o console, aperte e segure
o botão POWER por três segundos
e selecione "Opções de desligar".		 Unable to access microSD card.

Please turn off the console and reinsert
the microSD card.

You can turn the power off from Power Options in the Power Menu (hold the POWER Button for three seconds).		 L'accès à la carte microSD a échoué.

Éteignez la console, puis retirez et réinsérez la carte microSD.

Vous pouvez éteindre la console depuis les options d'alimentation, dans le menu d'alimentation (maintenez le bouton d'alimentation trois secondes pour l'ouvrir).		 Es konnte nicht auf die microSD Card zugegriffen werden.

Bitte schalte die Konsole aus, entferne die microSD Card und stecke sie erneut ein.

Du kannst die Konsole in den Power-Optionen im POWER-Menü ausschalten (POWER-Knopf 3 Sekunden gedrückt halten).		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta microSD.

Apaga la consola y vuelve a insertar la tarjeta.

Para apagar la consola, mantén pulsado
el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 Impossibile accedere alla scheda microSD.

Spegni la console e reinserisci la scheda microSD.

Per spegnere la console, seleziona Opzioni di spegnimento nel menu Power (tieni premuto il pulsante POWER per tre secondi).		 De microSD-kaart is niet toegankelijk.

Zet het systeem uit en voer de microSD-kaart opnieuw in.

Je kunt het systeem uitzetten via OPNIEUW OPSTARTEN / UITZETTEN in het POWER-menu (houd de POWER-knop drie seconden ingedrukt).		 Não é possível aceder ao cartão microSD.

Desliga a consola e volta a inserir o cartão microSD.

Podes desligar a consola a partir das opções de energia no menu Power (mantém premido o botão POWER durante três segundos).		 Не удается получить доступ к карте microSD.

Выключите консоль, извлеките и снова вставьте карту microSD.

Вы можете отключить питание в меню электропитания в разделе Параметры электропитания (нажмите и удерживайте кнопку POWER в течение трех секунд).		 SDカードにアクセスできませんでした。

本体の電源をOFFにしてから、SDカードを差しなおしてください。

電源OFFは、電源メニュー（電源ボタンを3秒長押し）の「電源オプション」からおこなえます。		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 無法存取SD卡。

請關閉主機電源後，重新插入SD卡。

關閉電源可到電源選單（長按電源鍵3秒）的「電源選項」執行。		 SD 카드에 액세스하지 못했습니다.

본체 전원을 끄고 나서 SD 카드를 다시 꽂아 주십시오.

전원 OFF는 전원 메뉴(전원 버튼 3초간 누르기)의 「전원 옵션」에서 할 수 있습니다.		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 OK
2002-2083 ResultSdCardAccessFailed Failed to access the SD card. Failed to access the SD card.
Check the following.
• Is the SD card inserted correctly?
　Turn the power off and re-insert the SD card.
• Is the SD card terminal part contaminated?
　If it is contaminated, wipe it of with a soft, dry cloth such as a glasses cleaning cloth. Wipe gently so as not to damage the terminals.
• Consider formatting the SD card.
　From the HOME Menu, select Settings > System > Formatting Options > Format microSD Card to format the SD card.
　If you format the SD card, all of the saved data on it is deleted. As needed, move screenshots and video data to the system memory or a PC in advance.
　The save data is saved in the system memory instead of on the SD card.
• If the preceding measures do not resolve the issue, there may be a problem with the SD card itself. Consider replacing the SD card.
　If replacing the SD card does not resolve the issue, the console might be defective.

Alternatively, excessive access to the storage media might have caused malfunctioning or data corruption.
For more information about access restrictions on storage media, see Guidelines > File system > Storage media > Limits on frequency and amount of writing to storage media, and also Limit on frequency of reading storage media.		 Unable to access microSD card.

전원 OFF는 전원 메뉴(전원 버튼 3초간 누르기)의 「전원 옵션」에서 할 수 있습니다.		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 OK
2002-2087 ResultSdCardAccessFailed Failed to access the SD card. Failed to access the SD card.
Check the following.
• Is the SD card inserted correctly?
　Turn the power off and re-insert the SD card.
• Is the SD card terminal part contaminated?
　If it is contaminated, wipe it of with a soft, dry cloth such as a glasses cleaning cloth. Wipe gently so as not to damage the terminals.
• Consider formatting the SD card.
　From the HOME Menu, select Settings > System > Formatting Options > Format microSD Card to format the SD card.
　If you format the SD card, all of the saved data on it is deleted. As needed, move screenshots and video data to the system memory or a PC in advance.
　The save data is saved in the system memory instead of on the SD card.
• If the preceding measures do not resolve the issue, there may be a problem with the SD card itself. Consider replacing the SD card.
　If replacing the SD card does not resolve the issue, the console might be defective.

Alternatively, excessive access to the storage media might have caused malfunctioning or data corruption.
For more information about access restrictions on storage media, see Guidelines > File system > Storage media > Limits on frequency and amount of writing to storage media, and also Limit on frequency of reading storage media.		 Unable to access microSD card.

Please turn off the console and reinsert
the microSD card.

You can turn the power off from Power Options in the Power Menu (hold the POWER Button for three seconds).		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta microSD.

Apaga la consola y vuelve a insertar la tarjeta.

Para apagar la consola, mantén oprimido
el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 L'accès à la carte microSD a échoué.

Éteignez la console, puis retirez et réinsérez la carte microSD.

Vous pouvez éteindre la console depuis les options d'alimentation, dans le menu d'alimentation (maintenez le bouton d'alimentation trois secondes pour l'ouvrir).		 Não foi possível acessar o cartão microSD.

Desligue o console e insira o cartão novamente.

Para desligar o console, aperte e segure
o botão POWER por três segundos
e selecione "Opções de desligar".		 Unable to access microSD card.

Please turn off the console and reinsert
the microSD card.

You can turn the power off from Power Options in the Power Menu (hold the POWER Button for three seconds).		 L'accès à la carte microSD a échoué.

Éteignez la console, puis retirez et réinsérez la carte microSD.

Vous pouvez éteindre la console depuis les options d'alimentation, dans le menu d'alimentation (maintenez le bouton d'alimentation trois secondes pour l'ouvrir).		 Es konnte nicht auf die microSD Card zugegriffen werden.

Bitte schalte die Konsole aus, entferne die microSD Card und stecke sie erneut ein.

Du kannst die Konsole in den Power-Optionen im POWER-Menü ausschalten (POWER-Knopf 3 Sekunden gedrückt halten).		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta microSD.

Apaga la consola y vuelve a insertar la tarjeta.

Para apagar la consola, mantén pulsado
el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 Impossibile accedere alla scheda microSD.

Spegni la console e reinserisci la scheda microSD.

Per spegnere la console, seleziona Opzioni di spegnimento nel menu Power (tieni premuto il pulsante POWER per tre secondi).		 De microSD-kaart is niet toegankelijk.

Zet het systeem uit en voer de microSD-kaart opnieuw in.

Je kunt het systeem uitzetten via OPNIEUW OPSTARTEN / UITZETTEN in het POWER-menu (houd de POWER-knop drie seconden ingedrukt).		 Não é possível aceder ao cartão microSD.

Desliga a consola e volta a inserir o cartão microSD.

Podes desligar a consola a partir das opções de energia no menu Power (mantém premido o botão POWER durante três segundos).		 Не удается получить доступ к карте microSD.

Выключите консоль, извлеките и снова вставьте карту microSD.

Вы можете отключить питание в меню электропитания в разделе Параметры электропитания (нажмите и удерживайте кнопку POWER в течение трех секунд).		 SDカードにアクセスできませんでした。

本体の電源をOFFにしてから、SDカードを差しなおしてください。

電源OFFは、電源メニュー（電源ボタンを3秒長押し）の「電源オプション」からおこなえます。		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 無法存取SD卡。

y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 Impossibile accedere alla scheda microSD.

Spegni la console e reinserisci la scheda microSD.

Per spegnere la console, seleziona Opzioni di spegnimento nel menu Power (tieni premuto il pulsante POWER per tre secondi).		 De microSD-kaart is niet toegankelijk.

Zet het systeem uit en voer de microSD-kaart opnieuw in.

Je kunt het systeem uitzetten via OPNIEUW OPSTARTEN / UITZETTEN in het POWER-menu (houd de POWER-knop drie seconden ingedrukt).		 Não é possível aceder ao cartão microSD.

Desliga a consola e volta a inserir o cartão microSD.

Podes desligar a consola a partir das opções de energia no menu Power (mantém premido o botão POWER durante três segundos).		 Не удается получить доступ к карте microSD.

Выключите консоль, извлеките и снова вставьте карту microSD.

Вы можете отключить питание в меню электропитания в разделе Параметры электропитания (нажмите и удерживайте кнопку POWER в течение трех секунд).		 SDカードにアクセスできませんでした。

本体の電源をOFFにしてから、SDカードを差しなおしてください。

電源OFFは、電源メニュー（電源ボタンを3秒長押し）の「電源オプション」からおこなえます。		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 無法存取SD卡。

請關閉主機電源後，重新插入SD卡。

關閉電源可到電源選單（長按電源鍵3秒）的「電源選項」執行。		 SD 카드에 액세스하지 못했습니다.

본체 전원을 끄고 나서 SD 카드를 다시 꽂아 주십시오.

전원 OFF는 전원 메뉴(전원 버튼 3초간 누르기)의 「전원 옵션」에서 할 수 있습니다.		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 OK
2002-2092 ResultSdCardAccessFailed Failed to access the SD card. Failed to access the SD card.
Check the following.
• Is the SD card inserted correctly?
　Turn the power off and re-insert the SD card.
• Is the SD card terminal part contaminated?
　If it is contaminated, wipe it of with a soft, dry cloth such as a glasses cleaning cloth. Wipe gently so as not to damage the terminals.
• Consider formatting the SD card.
　From the HOME Menu, select Settings > System > Formatting Options > Format microSD Card to format the SD card.
　If you format the SD card, all of the saved data on it is deleted. As needed, move screenshots and video data to the system memory or a PC in advance.
　The save data is saved in the system memory instead of on the SD card.
• If the preceding measures do not resolve the issue, there may be a problem with the SD card itself. Consider replacing the SD card.
　If replacing the SD card does not resolve the issue, the console might be defective.

Alternatively, excessive access to the storage media might have caused malfunctioning or data corruption.
For more information about access restrictions on storage media, see Guidelines > File system > Storage media > Limits on frequency and amount of writing to storage media, and also Limit on frequency of reading storage media.		 Unable to access microSD card.

Please turn off the console and reinsert
the microSD card.

You can turn the power off from Power Options in the Power Menu (hold the POWER Button for three seconds).		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta microSD.

For more information about access restrictions on storage media, see Guidelines > File system > Storage media > Limits on frequency and amount of writing to storage media, and also Limit on frequency of reading storage media.		 Unable to access microSD card.

Please turn off the console and reinsert
the microSD card.

You can turn the power off from Power Options in the Power Menu (hold the POWER Button for three seconds).		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta microSD.

Apaga la consola y vuelve a insertar la tarjeta.

Para apagar la consola, mantén oprimido
el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 L'accès à la carte microSD a échoué.

Éteignez la console, puis retirez et réinsérez la carte microSD.

Vous pouvez éteindre la console depuis les options d'alimentation, dans le menu d'alimentation (maintenez le bouton d'alimentation trois secondes pour l'ouvrir).		 Não foi possível acessar o cartão microSD.

Para desligar o console, aperte e segure
o botão POWER por três segundos
e selecione "Opções de desligar".		 Unable to access microSD card.

Please turn off the console and reinsert
the microSD card.

You can turn the power off from Power Options in the Power Menu (hold the POWER Button for three seconds).		 L'accès à la carte microSD a échoué.

Vous pouvez éteindre la console depuis les options d'alimentation, dans le menu d'alimentation (maintenez le bouton d'alimentation trois secondes pour l'ouvrir).		 Es konnte nicht auf die microSD Card zugegriffen werden.

Du kannst die Konsole in den Power-Optionen im POWER-Menü ausschalten (POWER-Knopf 3 Sekunden gedrückt halten).		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta microSD.

Apaga la consola y vuelve a insertar la tarjeta.

Para apagar la consola, mantén pulsado
el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 Impossibile accedere alla scheda microSD.

Spegni la console e reinserisci la scheda microSD.

Per spegnere la console, seleziona Opzioni di spegnimento nel menu Power (tieni premuto il pulsante POWER per tre secondi).		 De microSD-kaart is niet toegankelijk.

Zet het systeem uit en voer de microSD-kaart opnieuw in.

Je kunt het systeem uitzetten via OPNIEUW OPSTARTEN / UITZETTEN in het POWER-menu (houd de POWER-knop drie seconden ingedrukt).		 Não é possível aceder ao cartão microSD.

Desliga a consola e volta a inserir o cartão microSD.

Podes desligar a consola a partir das opções de energia no menu Power (mantém premido o botão POWER durante três segundos).		 Не удается получить доступ к карте microSD.

Выключите консоль, извлеките и снова вставьте карту microSD.

Вы можете отключить питание в меню электропитания в разделе Параметры электропитания (нажмите и удерживайте кнопку POWER в течение трех секунд).		 SDカードにアクセスできませんでした。

本体の電源をOFFにしてから、SDカードを差しなおしてください。

電源OFFは、電源メニュー（電源ボタンを3秒長押し）の「電源オプション」からおこなえます。		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 無法存取SD卡。

請關閉主機電源後，重新插入SD卡。

關閉電源可到電源選單（長按電源鍵3秒）的「電源選項」執行。		 SD 카드에 액세스하지 못했습니다.

본체 전원을 끄고 나서 SD 카드를 다시 꽂아 주십시오.

전원 OFF는 전원 메뉴(전원 버튼 3초간 누르기)의 「전원 옵션」에서 할 수 있습니다.		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 OK
2002-2097 ResultSdCardAccessFailed Failed to access the SD card. Failed to access the SD card.
Check the following.
• Is the SD card inserted correctly?
　Turn the power off and re-insert the SD card.
• Is the SD card terminal part contaminated?
　If it is contaminated, wipe it of with a soft, dry cloth such as a glasses cleaning cloth. Wipe gently so as not to damage the terminals.
• Consider formatting the SD card.
　From the HOME Menu, select Settings > System > Formatting Options > Format microSD Card to format the SD card.
　If you format the SD card, all of the saved data on it is deleted. As needed, move screenshots and video data to the system memory or a PC in advance.
　The save data is saved in the system memory instead of on the SD card.
• If the preceding measures do not resolve the issue, there may be a problem with the SD card itself. Consider replacing the SD card.
　If replacing the SD card does not resolve the issue, the console might be defective.

Alternatively, excessive access to the storage media might have caused malfunctioning or data corruption.

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 無法存取SD卡。

請關閉主機電源後，重新插入SD卡。

關閉電源可到電源選單（長按電源鍵3秒）的「電源選項」執行。		 SD 카드에 액세스하지 못했습니다.

본체 전원을 끄고 나서 SD 카드를 다시 꽂아 주십시오.

전원 OFF는 전원 메뉴(전원 버튼 3초간 누르기)의 「전원 옵션」에서 할 수 있습니다.		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 OK
2002-2132 ResultSdCardAccessFailed Failed to access the SD card. Failed to access the SD card.
Check the following.
• Is the SD card inserted correctly?
　Turn the power off and re-insert the SD card.
• Is the SD card terminal part contaminated?
　If it is contaminated, wipe it of with a soft, dry cloth such as a glasses cleaning cloth. Wipe gently so as not to damage the terminals.
• Consider formatting the SD card.
　From the HOME Menu, select Settings > System > Formatting Options > Format microSD Card to format the SD card.
　If you format the SD card, all of the saved data on it is deleted. As needed, move screenshots and video data to the system memory or a PC in advance.
　The save data is saved in the system memory instead of on the SD card.
• If the preceding measures do not resolve the issue, there may be a problem with the SD card itself. Consider replacing the SD card.
　If replacing the SD card does not resolve the issue, the console might be defective.

Alternatively, excessive access to the storage media might have caused malfunctioning or data corruption.
For more information about access restrictions on storage media, see Guidelines > File system > Storage media > Limits on frequency and amount of writing to storage media, and also Limit on frequency of reading storage media.		 Unable to access microSD card.

Please turn off the console and reinsert
the microSD card.

You can turn the power off from Power Options in the Power Menu (hold the POWER Button for three seconds).		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta microSD.

Apaga la consola y vuelve a insertar la tarjeta.

Para apagar la consola, mantén oprimido
el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 L'accès à la carte microSD a échoué.

Éteignez la console, puis retirez et réinsérez la carte microSD.

Vous pouvez éteindre la console depuis les options d'alimentation, dans le menu d'alimentation (maintenez le bouton d'alimentation trois secondes pour l'ouvrir).		 Não foi possível acessar o cartão microSD.

Desligue o console e insira o cartão novamente.

Para desligar o console, aperte e segure
o botão POWER por três segundos
e selecione "Opções de desligar".		 Unable to access microSD card.

Please turn off the console and reinsert
the microSD card.

You can turn the power off from Power Options in the Power Menu (hold the POWER Button for three seconds).		 L'accès à la carte microSD a échoué.

Éteignez la console, puis retirez et réinsérez la carte microSD.

Vous pouvez éteindre la console depuis les options d'alimentation, dans le menu d'alimentation (maintenez le bouton d'alimentation trois secondes pour l'ouvrir).		 Es konnte nicht auf die microSD Card zugegriffen werden.

Bitte schalte die Konsole aus, entferne die microSD Card und stecke sie erneut ein.

Du kannst die Konsole in den Power-Optionen im POWER-Menü ausschalten (POWER-Knopf 3 Sekunden gedrückt halten).		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta microSD.

Apaga la consola y vuelve a insertar la tarjeta.

Para apagar la consola, mantén pulsado
el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 Impossibile accedere alla scheda microSD.

Spegni la console e reinserisci la scheda microSD.

Per spegnere la console, seleziona Opzioni di spegnimento nel menu Power (tieni premuto il pulsante POWER per tre secondi).		 De microSD-kaart is niet toegankelijk.

Zet het systeem uit en voer de microSD-kaart opnieuw in.

Je kunt het systeem uitzetten via OPNIEUW OPSTARTEN / UITZETTEN in het POWER-menu (houd de POWER-knop drie seconden ingedrukt).		 Não é possível aceder ao cartão microSD.

Desliga a consola e volta a inserir o cartão microSD.

Podes desligar a consola a partir das opções de energia no menu Power (mantém premido o botão POWER durante três segundos).		 Не удается получить доступ к карте microSD.

Выключите консоль, извлеките и снова вставьте карту microSD.

Вы можете отключить питание в меню электропитания в разделе Параметры электропитания (нажмите и удерживайте кнопку POWER в течение трех секунд).		 SDカードにアクセスできませんでした。

本体の電源をOFFにしてから、SDカードを差しなおしてください。

電源OFFは、電源メニュー（電源ボタンを3秒長押し）の「電源オプション」からおこなえます。		 无法读取SD卡。

Vous pouvez éteindre la console depuis les options d'alimentation, dans le menu d'alimentation (maintenez le bouton d'alimentation trois secondes pour l'ouvrir).		 Es konnte nicht auf die microSD Card zugegriffen werden.

Bitte schalte die Konsole aus, entferne die microSD Card und stecke sie erneut ein.

Du kannst die Konsole in den Power-Optionen im POWER-Menü ausschalten (POWER-Knopf 3 Sekunden gedrückt halten).		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta microSD.

Apaga la consola y vuelve a insertar la tarjeta.

Para apagar la consola, mantén pulsado
el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 Impossibile accedere alla scheda microSD.

Spegni la console e reinserisci la scheda microSD.

Per spegnere la console, seleziona Opzioni di spegnimento nel menu Power (tieni premuto il pulsante POWER per tre secondi).		 De microSD-kaart is niet toegankelijk.

Zet het systeem uit en voer de microSD-kaart opnieuw in.

Je kunt het systeem uitzetten via OPNIEUW OPSTARTEN / UITZETTEN in het POWER-menu (houd de POWER-knop drie seconden ingedrukt).		 Não é possível aceder ao cartão microSD.

Desliga a consola e volta a inserir o cartão microSD.

Podes desligar a consola a partir das opções de energia no menu Power (mantém premido o botão POWER durante três segundos).		 Не удается получить доступ к карте microSD.

Выключите консоль, извлеките и снова вставьте карту microSD.

Вы можете отключить питание в меню электропитания в разделе Параметры электропитания (нажмите и удерживайте кнопку POWER в течение трех секунд).		 SDカードにアクセスできませんでした。

本体の電源をOFFにしてから、SDカードを差しなおしてください。

電源OFFは、電源メニュー（電源ボタンを3秒長押し）の「電源オプション」からおこなえます。		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 無法存取SD卡。

請關閉主機電源後，重新插入SD卡。

關閉電源可到電源選單（長按電源鍵3秒）的「電源選項」執行。		 SD 카드에 액세스하지 못했습니다.

본체 전원을 끄고 나서 SD 카드를 다시 꽂아 주십시오.

전원 OFF는 전원 메뉴(전원 버튼 3초간 누르기)의 「전원 옵션」에서 할 수 있습니다.		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 OK
2002-2192 ResultSdCardAccessFailed Failed to access the SD card. Failed to access the SD card.
Check the following.
• Is the SD card inserted correctly?
　Turn the power off and re-insert the SD card.
• Is the SD card terminal part contaminated?
　If it is contaminated, wipe it of with a soft, dry cloth such as a glasses cleaning cloth. Wipe gently so as not to damage the terminals.
• Consider formatting the SD card.
　From the HOME Menu, select Settings > System > Formatting Options > Format microSD Card to format the SD card.
　If you format the SD card, all of the saved data on it is deleted. As needed, move screenshots and video data to the system memory or a PC in advance.
　The save data is saved in the system memory instead of on the SD card.
• If the preceding measures do not resolve the issue, there may be a problem with the SD card itself. Consider replacing the SD card.
　If replacing the SD card does not resolve the issue, the console might be defective.

Alternatively, excessive access to the storage media might have caused malfunctioning or data corruption.
For more information about access restrictions on storage media, see Guidelines > File system > Storage media > Limits on frequency and amount of writing to storage media, and also Limit on frequency of reading storage media.		 Unable to access microSD card.

　Turn the power off and re-insert the SD card.
• Is the SD card terminal part contaminated?
　If it is contaminated, wipe it of with a soft, dry cloth such as a glasses cleaning cloth. Wipe gently so as not to damage the terminals.
• Consider formatting the SD card.
　From the HOME Menu, select Settings > System > Formatting Options > Format microSD Card to format the SD card.
　If you format the SD card, all of the saved data on it is deleted. As needed, move screenshots and video data to the system memory or a PC in advance.
　The save data is saved in the system memory instead of on the SD card.
• If the preceding measures do not resolve the issue, there may be a problem with the SD card itself. Consider replacing the SD card.
　If replacing the SD card does not resolve the issue, the console might be defective.

Alternatively, excessive access to the storage media might have caused malfunctioning or data corruption.
For more information about access restrictions on storage media, see Guidelines > File system > Storage media > Limits on frequency and amount of writing to storage media, and also Limit on frequency of reading storage media.		 Unable to access microSD card.

Please turn off the console and reinsert
the microSD card.

You can turn the power off from Power Options in the Power Menu (hold the POWER Button for three seconds).		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta microSD.

Apaga la consola y vuelve a insertar la tarjeta.

Para apagar la consola, mantén oprimido
el botón POWER durante tres segundos
y selecciona "Opciones de apagado".		 L'accès à la carte microSD a échoué.

Vous pouvez éteindre la console depuis les options d'alimentation, dans le menu d'alimentation (maintenez le bouton d'alimentation trois secondes pour l'ouvrir).		 Não foi possível acessar o cartão microSD.

o botão POWER por três segundos
e selecione "Opções de desligar".		 Unable to access microSD card.

Zet het systeem uit en voer de microSD-kaart opnieuw in.

Je kunt het systeem uitzetten via OPNIEUW OPSTARTEN / UITZETTEN in het POWER-menu (houd de POWER-knop drie seconden ingedrukt).		 Não é possível aceder ao cartão microSD.

Desliga a consola e volta a inserir o cartão microSD.

Podes desligar a consola a partir das opções de energia no menu Power (mantém premido o botão POWER durante três segundos).		 Не удается получить доступ к карте microSD.

Выключите консоль, извлеките и снова вставьте карту microSD.

Вы можете отключить питание в меню электропитания в разделе Параметры электропитания (нажмите и удерживайте кнопку POWER в течение трех секунд).		 SDカードにアクセスできませんでした。

本体の電源をOFFにしてから、SDカードを差しなおしてください。

電源OFFは、電源メニュー（電源ボタンを3秒長押し）の「電源オプション」からおこなえます。		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 無法存取SD卡。

請關閉主機電源後，重新插入SD卡。

關閉電源可到電源選單（長按電源鍵3秒）的「電源選項」執行。		 SD 카드에 액세스하지 못했습니다.

본체 전원을 끄고 나서 SD 카드를 다시 꽂아 주십시오.

전원 OFF는 전원 메뉴(전원 버튼 3초간 누르기)의 「전원 옵션」에서 할 수 있습니다.		 无法读取SD卡。

请关闭主机电源后，重新插入SD卡。

可以在电源菜单（长按电源键3秒）的“电源选项”中关闭电源。		 OK

2002-2500 ResultGameCardAccessFailed Failed to access game card. Failed to access game card.
Check the following.
• Is the game card inserted properly?
　Re-insert the game card and check whether the situation resolves.
• Is the game card terminal contaminated?
　If it is contaminated, wipe it of with a soft, dry cloth such as a glasses cleaning cloth. Wipe gently so as not to damage the terminals.
• If the game card produces the same error with other consoles, the game card might be defective.
• If the game card works normally with other consoles, and the same error occurs when another game card is inserted into the specific console, that console might be defective.

Alternatively, excessive access to the storage media might have caused malfunctioning or data corruption.
For more information about access restrictions on storage media, see Guidelines > File system > Storage media > Limits on frequency and amount of writing to storage media, and also Limit on frequency of reading storage media.		 Unable to access game card.

Please reinsert the game card.		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta de juego.

게임 카드를 다시 꽂아 주십시오.		 无法读取游戏卡。

请重新插入游戏卡。		 OK
2002-2504 ResultGameCardAccessFailed Failed to access game card. Failed to access game card.
Check the following.
• Is the game card inserted properly?
　Re-insert the game card and check whether the situation resolves.
• Is the game card terminal contaminated?
　If it is contaminated, wipe it of with a soft, dry cloth such as a glasses cleaning cloth. Wipe gently so as not to damage the terminals.
• If the game card produces the same error with other consoles, the game card might be defective.
• If the game card works normally with other consoles, and the same error occurs when another game card is inserted into the specific console, that console might be defective.

Alternatively, excessive access to the storage media might have caused malfunctioning or data corruption.
For more information about access restrictions on storage media, see Guidelines > File system > Storage media > Limits on frequency and amount of writing to storage media, and also Limit on frequency of reading storage media.		 Unable to access game card.

Please reinsert the game card.		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta de juego.

Vuelve a insertarla.		 L'accès à la carte de jeu a échoué.

Retirez-la, puis réinsérez-la.		 Não foi possível acessar o cartão de jogo.

Insira-o novamente.		 Unable to access game card.

Please reinsert the game card.		 L'accès à la carte de jeu a échoué.

Retirez-la, puis réinsérez-la.		 Es konnte nicht auf die Softwarekarte zugegriffen werden.

Bitte entferne die Softwarekarte und stecke sie erneut ein.		 No se ha podido acceder a la tarjeta
de juego.

Vuelve a insertarla.		 Impossibile accedere alla scheda di gioco.

Inseriscila nuovamente.		 De gamecard is niet toegankelijk.

Voer de gamecard opnieuw in.		 Não é possível aceder ao cartão de jogo.

Volta a inseri-lo.		 Не удается получить доступ к игровой карте.

Извлеките и снова вставьте игровую карту.		 ゲームカードにアクセスできませんでした。

ゲームカードを差しなおしてください。		 无法读取游戏卡。

请重新插入游戏卡。		 無法存取遊戲卡。
